You'll receive a custom plan with priorities, expected results, and timeline. Once approved, we start within 24 hours — no lengthy onboarding."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"What happens during the discovery call?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"We'll walk through your current operations, identify bottlenecks, and pinpoint which automations would deliver the fastest ROI. You'll leave with a clear picture of what's possible and what we'd prioritize first — no pressure to commit on the call."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"What is the typical project timeline?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Most projects take 2-4 weeks from kickoff to launch. Complex multi-system integrations may take 4-6 weeks. We'll give you a specific timeline on the discovery call based on your scope — and we hit our deadlines."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Who will work on my automation project?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Your project is led by senior automation architects, not junior developers. Our team has designed and deployed 100+ production systems and you work directly with the experts building your automations—no account managers or middlemen. We collaborate daily with businesses, so we understand real operational challenges as well as the technical details."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"What's your pricing?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Our Automation Club is a flat monthly retainer for unlimited automation requests with 48-hour turnaround — no scoping delays, no surprise invoices. It's ideal for companies with ongoing automation needs. For one-off projects, we also offer fixed-scope engagements. Book a discovery call to discuss pricing and see which option fits your situation."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"What is your policy on post-launch adjustments?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Every project includes 30 days of free adjustments after launch — if something isn't working as expected, we fix it at no extra cost. After that, changes are handled with transparent pricing based on scope. Most clients find their systems run smoothly with minimal tweaks once dialed in."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Do the automations need maintenance?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"The automations we build are designed to work reliably without constant attention. That said, the tools you use — CRMs, APIs, AI models — release updates and new features over time. When that happens, systems may need adjustments. We offer ongoing maintenance plans so you're never left scrambling when something changes."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Is my business data secure with PulpSense?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Yes. All connections are encrypted, we use secure authentication methods (and never store your passwords), and we follow GDPR-compliant data handling practices. We sign NDAs with every client and can accommodate specific compliance requirements — just let us know on the discovery call."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Do I need technical knowledge to use the automations?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Not at all. Every automation comes with documentation, video walkthroughs, and a handover session so your team knows exactly how to use it. If questions come up after launch, we're available to help."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"What tools and platforms do you integrate with?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"We integrate with all major business tools — Salesforce, HubSpot, Monday.com, Slack, QuickBooks, Stripe, Airtable, and hundreds more. For custom or legacy systems, we can usually find a way through APIs or workarounds. If you're unsure about a specific tool, just ask."}}]}</script><section id="faq" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-10 pb-10 w-full relative"><div class="border-b w-full h-full p-10
